# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-11-15 through 2026-11-21  
**Updated:** 2026-11-15T11:06:24+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-03-15

## Best date plans

### 2026-11-15 — Renegade Craft: New York Fall/Winter Fair + Altbau Bakery

**Renegade Craft: New York Fall/Winter Fair, then a meal.** Artisan bread and pastries at Altbau Bakery. It is 2.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=125+W+18th+Street&destination=184+N+8th+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.renegadecraft.com/event/new-york-fall/) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-11-17 — SKATE at Domino Park + Altbau Bakery

**SKATE at Domino Park, then a meal.** Artisan bread and pastries at Altbau Bakery. It is a 13-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=South+3rd+Street+between+Kent+Avenue+and+River+Street&destination=184+N+8th+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.dominopark.com/ice-skating) · [Event booking](https://www.dominopark.com/ice-skating/buy-tickets) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-11-16 — Azar + Bodega Cats of New York — book launch with Dan Rimada

**Dinner, then Bodega Cats of New York — book launch with Dan Rimada.** Persian street grill at Azar. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Bodega Cats of New York — book launch with Dan Rimada starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=740+Driggs+Avenue&destination=28+Adams+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://powerhousearena.com/events/book-launch-bodega-cats-of-new-york-by-dan-rimada/) · [Event booking](https://events.ticketleap.com/tickets/powerhousebookstores/book-launch-bodega-cats-of-new-york-by-dan-rimada) · [Restaurant](https://azar.nyc/)

### 2026-11-15 — Rest in Pace + Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café

**Rest in Pace, then a meal.** Seasonal American at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café. It is 1.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=500+25th+Street&destination=Brooklyn+Botanic+Garden%2C+Palm+House+on+Lily+Pool+Terrace%3B+enter+at+990+Washington+Avenue&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.green-wood.com/event/rest-in-pace-green-house-2/2026-11-15/) · [Restaurant](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/yellow-magnolia-cafe?date=2026-11-20&seats=2)

### 2026-11-21 — Barnard/Columbia Dances at New York Live Arts — Saturday + Casa Oaxaca

**Barnard/Columbia Dances at New York Live Arts — Saturday, then a meal.** Contemporary Mexican and Oaxacan at Casa Oaxaca. It is 2.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=219+West+19th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10011&destination=301+South+End+Avenue&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://newyorklivearts.org/event/barnard-columbia-dances-at-new-york-live-arts-5/) · [Event booking](https://newyorklivearts.my.salesforce-sites.com/ticket/#/events/a0SVt00000gDW57MAG) · [Restaurant](https://www.casaoaxacany.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.casaoaxacany.com/reservations)

## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 15, 2026: lunch deals, happy hours, recent openings and upcoming holiday menus. Date-specific offers are distinguished from everyday service; availability is not guaranteed.

Dining research: 2026-11-15T10:44:10+00:00

No Michelin awards are added. GUI's own structured data describes it as Michelin Selected, which is not a star claim. Its linked Michelin page returned no usable content, so independent confirmation was unavailable: https://guide.michelin.com/us/en/new-york-state/new-york/restaurant/gui-1207502.

### GUI's $8 three-course lunch upgrade

Deals & happy hours — GUI Steakhouse

Add a lunch-sized salad and a scoop of ice cream or sorbet to an eligible main. The mushroom-and-tofu pot rice provides the lowest-priced listed starting point.

Price: $8 upgrade; eligible mains include mushroom-and-tofu pot rice $19, kimchi wagyu fried rice $21 and prime galbi pot rice $23.  
Schedule: Daily, noon–2:30pm.  
Conditions: Upgrade applies to Prime Rib Dip and Lunch Favorites, not every entree. Reservation requirement, tax, gratuity and service charges are unstated. No offer end date or holiday exception was shown.  
[Source](https://guinyc.com/gui-lunch-menu)

### GUI's $65 three-course menu runs until closing

Deals & happy hours — GUI Steakhouse

Despite its pre-theater label, the menu is available throughout dinner. Main-course choices include hanger steak frites, branzino, chicken and spicy rigatoni.

Price: $65 per person for three courses.  
Schedule: Daily, 4pm–close; posted closing times are 10pm Monday–Saturday and 9pm Sunday.  
Conditions: Choose one dish from each course. Reservation requirement, tax, gratuity and service charges are unstated. No published end date or holiday exception was found.  
[Source](https://guinyc.com/gui-pre-theater-menu)

### Amélie's daily oysters, wine flights and $26 early-bird meal

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

A flexible early-evening stop for oysters and wine, or a burger or mussels meal with a drink.

Price: Oysters $1.50 each; six oysters and a glass of wine $19; three-wine flight $14; selected cocktails $10; beer $7; early-bird burger-and-fries or moules frites with beer or a glass of wine $26.  
Schedule: Food daily 4–6pm; drinks from opening until 6pm.  
Conditions: Only listed happy-hour selections qualify. Wine flights become $19 after 6pm. Oyster minimum, reservation requirement, tax, gratuity and service charges are unstated on this menu. Holiday exceptions are unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/happy-hour)

### Amélie's $26 weekday two-course lunch

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

A weekday bistro lunch with a bread basket included and optional wine.

Price: $26 for two courses; wine of the day +$10; burger +$5; steak frites or Niçoise +$8.  
Schedule: Monday–Friday, noon–3:30pm; not offered on Sunday 2026-11-15.  
Conditions: Tax and gratuity explicitly excluded. Extra bread basket $3; other supplements are listed. The page calls this two courses despite displaying three course categories; the permitted pairing of categories is not explicit. Reservation requirement and holiday exceptions are unknown.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/bistro)

### Amélie's $49 three-course prix fixe

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

A separate three-course option for a table willing to order the prix fixe together.

Price: $49 for three courses; steak supplement +$8.  
Schedule: Offer-specific days and hours are not stated.  
Conditions: The entire table must select the prix fixe. Tax and gratuity excluded; additional menu supplements may apply. Reservation requirement, service charges and holiday availability are unknown.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/prix-fixe)

### Miss Lily's $25 boozy-brunch add-on

Deals & happy hours — Miss Lily's 7A

Selected unlimited brunch cocktails accompany a separately purchased Signature Dish, with options including coconut pancakes and Jamaican breakfast.

Price: $25 per person drinks add-on; Signature Dishes include $18 coconut pancakes and $23 Jamaican breakfast.  
Schedule: Saturday and Sunday brunch, noon–4pm.  
Conditions: A Signature Dish purchase is required; $25 covers the drinks add-on, not a complete brunch. Eligible drinks are Pure Passion, Rum Punch, Coco-Lemon Drop and One Love Bellini. Duration and whole-table requirements are unstated. The restaurant's October 5, 2026 dinner PDF states that 18% gratuity is automatically added and additional tips are optional; the brunch HTML does not establish whether that charge also applies at brunch. Tax and other charges remain unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://v1-misslilys.getbento.com/east-village-7a/)

### The Half Pint's November 15–21 food, drinks and brunch specials

Deals & happy hours — The Half Pint

The dated calendar lists $5 burgers November 16, $5 egg rolls November 17, $5 fish tacos and $25 wine bottles November 18, and a Miller High Life buy-one-get-one offer November 19.

Price: $5 listed food specials; $25 wine bottles November 18; Miller High Life buy one, get one November 19, base price unstated; brunch drinks $28 per person with a separately purchased entree.  
Schedule: November 16–18 entries: 4–11pm. November 19 entry: 4pm–2am. Brunch November 15 and 21: 11am–3:30pm, with 90 minutes of bottomless cocktails. Weekday happy hour is separately listed noon–7pm.  
Conditions: Brunch requires an entree purchase. Food portions, wine labels and bottle sizes, beer serving sizes, BOGO limits, additional purchase restrictions, tax, gratuity and service charges are unstated. Calendar-entry hours are not the same as the separately advertised happy-hour window.  
From: 2026-11-15  
Through: 2026-11-21  
[Source](https://thehalfpint.com/specials)

### Frenzie's late-night pizza-and-drinks combinations

Deals & happy hours — Frenzie

Share a cheese pie with a beer pitcher or a pair of cocktails during the late-night service.

Price: Cheese pie and Narragansett pitcher $40; cheese pie and two daiquiris $45; cheese pie and two martinis $50; pepperoni +$4.  
Schedule: Thursday–Saturday after 10pm; ending time unstated. Not offered Sunday 2026-11-15.  
Conditions: Walk-ins only. Menu subject to seasonal availability. Takeout eligibility, tax, gratuity and service charges are unstated; do not assume the combinations are available for takeout. Holiday hours remain unverified.  
[Source](https://www.frenzienyc.com/menus/)

### La Compagnie Flatiron's Alexia Duchêne collaboration

Special menus — La Compagnie Flatiron

Consulting chef Alexia Duchêne brings seasonal French-inspired dishes to the Flatiron wine bar as part of Experimental's November 8 menu launch.

Schedule: Flatiron venue hours: Monday–Wednesday 5pm–midnight; Thursday–Saturday 5pm–1am; Sunday 5–11pm. Kitchen hours unknown.  
Conditions: An ongoing chef-menu collaboration, not a one-night pop-up or fixed-price dinner. Menu prices, reservation requirement and holiday exceptions were not established.  
From: 2026-11-08  
[Source](https://www.experimentalgroup.com/new-york/la-compagnie-flatiron)

### The Otter's November 20–26 Friendsgiving specials

Special menus — The Otter

A bounded holiday menu featuring stuffed turkey roulade with mashed potatoes and cranberry, warm pecan pie and apple sorbet.

Schedule: November 20–26; full daily service hours unknown.  
Conditions: Seasonal specials, not a verified discount or prix fixe. No price, included course count, minimum spend, cancellation terms or tax/tip/service charges appear in the readable experience description. Booking requirement and availability are unconfirmed; the booking interface does not establish full service hours.  
From: 2026-11-20  
Through: 2026-11-26

### Le Rock's $185 Thanksgiving menu

Special menus — Le Rock

The Thanksgiving menu includes opening snacks, appetizer and main-course choices, shared sides and dessert. Alternatives to heritage turkey include halibut, filet au poivre and wild-mushroom rice pilaf.

Price: $185 per person.  
Schedule: Thanksgiving, November 26; service times unknown.  
Conditions: For November 26, not November 15: posted regular hours do not include Sundays in November. Reservation availability, tax, gratuity and service charges are unknown. Supplements and children's pricing were not independently established from the readable official menu.  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/)

### Casa Oaxaca opened November 12; Thanksgiving specialties announced

New openings — Casa Oaxaca

Opened November 12 for dinner and weekend brunch. Thanksgiving specialties include guajillo-orange turkey with mole negro, piloncillo sweet potatoes, chorizo-cornbread stuffing and pecan tart with cajeta.

Schedule: Dinner and weekend brunch; regular hours unconfirmed. Thanksgiving, November 26, from 4pm; holiday closing time unstated.  
Conditions: Thanksgiving prices and ordering format are unstated; no set-menu price or discount is claimed. Reservations are encouraged on the holiday page, but availability and mandatory-booking status are unknown. Advertised happy hour lacked readable terms and is not presented as a deal.

2026-11-25 · 7:30 PM · theater · Polonsky Shakespeare Center · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 72.0  
Daniel Fish directs Sophokles's tragedy in Anne Carson's translation, with choral music by Ted Hearne and Tina Benko as Elektra.
**Timing:** November 25 at 7:30pm is a listed performance without a sold-out label. The final performance is December 6 at 7:30pm.
**Practical note:** No late seating. Several dates are sold out or marked limited to no availability; a missing warning is not confirmation of a particular seat.
**Why now:** Verified scarcity already affects the remaining run, including its final week.

2026-11-29 · 3:00 PM · theater · Run-wide advertised tickets from $25, plus fees. · BAM Harvey Theater · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 62.0  
Tiago Rodrigues's North American premiere follows a daughter who leaves Earth for Mars and the father she leaves behind. Alison Dechamps and Adama Diop perform on a rotating stage.

**Practical note:** Performed in French with English supertitles. Advertised $25 starting tickets incur $11 per ticket in online or phone fees; closing-performance inventory and pricing may differ.
**Why now:** The visiting production leaves BAM in two weeks; the alert is its confirmed ending, not its already-past ticket release.
**Book soon:** BAM confirms a November 18–29 engagement, and its official ticket calendar identifies November 29 at 3pm as the final performance.
